01599: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
01600:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
01601:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
01602:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
01604: }

00197: /**
00198:  * ✅ Normaliza "horário" para HH:MM
00199:  */
00200: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
00201:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
00202:   if (!s0) return "";
00203: 
00204:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
00205: 
00206:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
00207:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
00208: 
00209:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
00210:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
00211: 
00212:   const mHm = s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);

00796: export async function getKingBoundsByUf(ufOrObj) {
00797:   const uf = String(extractUfParam(ufOrObj) || "").trim();
00798:   if (!uf) throw new Error("Parâmetro obrigatório: uf");
00799: 
00800:   const apiTry = await fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj);
00801:   if (apiTry?.ok) {
00802:     const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
00803:     const floored = applyBoundsFloor(scopeKey, {
00804:       minYmd: apiTry.minYmd,
00805:       maxYmd: apiTry.maxYmd,
00806:     });
00807: 
00808:     return {
00809:       ok: true,
00810:       uf,
00811: 
00812:       minYmd: floored.minYmd,
00813:       maxYmd: floored.maxYmd,

01004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
01005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
01006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
01007: 
01008:   const parts = prizes
01009:     .map((p) => {
01010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
01011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
01012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
01013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
01014:         pos
01015:       );
01016: 
01017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
01018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
01019:     })
01020:     .filter(Boolean)

01000: /* =========================
01001:    API: Day
01002: ========================= */
01003: 
01004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
01005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
01006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
01007: 
01008:   const parts = prizes
01009:     .map((p) => {
01010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
01011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
01012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
01013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
01014:         pos
01015:       );
01016: 
01017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
01018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
01019:     })
01020:     .filter(Boolean)
01021:     .sort();
01022: 
01023:   return parts.join("|");
01024: }

01027:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
01028:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
01029:   );


LINHAS 1093 - 1143

01093:   return sortDrawsLocal(Array.from(byKey.values()));
01094: }
01095: 
01096: function drawsCacheKeyDay({ scopeKey, ymd, positionsArr, hourFilter }) {
01097:   const p = positionsArr && positionsArr.length ? positionsArr.join(",") : "all";
01098:   const h =
01099:     hourFilter?.kind === "bucket"
01100:       ? `bucket:${hourFilter.bucket}`
01101:       : hourFilter?.kind === "exact"
01102:       ? `exact:${hourFilter.hhmm}`
01103:       : "all";
01104:   return `day::${scopeKey}::${ymd}::pos=${p}::h=${h}`;
01105: }

01107: export async function getKingResultsByDate({
01108:   uf,
01109:   date,
01110:   closeHour = null,
01111:   closeHourBucket = null,
01112:   positions = null,
01113: }) {
01114:   if (!uf || !date) throw new Error("Parâmetros obrigatórios: uf e date");
01115: 
01116:   const scopeKey = canonicalScopeKey(uf);
01117: 
01118:   const positionsArr = normalizePositions(positions);
01119:   const ymdDate = normalizeToYMD(date);
01120:   if (!ymdDate) return [];
01121: 
01122:   const hourFilter = resolveHourFilter({ closeHour, closeHourBucket });
01123: 
01124:   const dayKey = drawsCacheKeyDay({
01125:     scopeKey,
01126:     ymd: ymdDate,
01127:     positionsArr,
01128:     hourFilter,
01129:   });
01130: 
01131:   const forceFreshServer = shouldForceFreshServerRead(ymdDate);
01132:   const allowMemoryCache = !forceFreshServer;
01133:

01599: function hourToNumSafe(hhmm) {
01600:   const s = normalizeHourLike(hhmm);
01601:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
01602:   if (!m) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01603:   return Number(m[1]) * 100 + Number(m[2]);
01604: }

00264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {

00274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
00275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
00276: 
00277:   const raw =
00278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";

00280: 
00281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
00282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
00283:   }
00284: 
00285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
00286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
00287:   }
00288: 
00289:   return true;

01050:     ? a.prizes.length

01057:     : 0;

00257: /* =========================
00258:    ✅ FIX DEFINITIVO: filtro de horário (bucket vs exato)
00259:    - bucket SOMENTE via closeHourBucket
00260:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour
00261:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")
00262: ========================= */
00263: 
00264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
00265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
00266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
00267: 
00268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
00269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
00270: 
00271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
00272: }
00273: 
00274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
00275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
00276: 
00277:   const raw =
00278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
00279:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
00280: 
00281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
00282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
00283:   }
00284: 
00285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
00286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
00287:   }
00288: 
00289:   return true;
00290: }
